党委书记、董事长：李大鹏

Party Secretary and Board Chairman: Li Dapeng

# 董事长致辞

2021 年，齐商银行在各级党委政府和监管部门的正确领导及社会各界的大力支持下，坚持稳中求进工作总基调，拼搏进取、攻坚克难，实现了规模、质量、效益的协调发展，赢得了广大客户、股东和社会各界的认可赞誉。截至 2021 年末，全行资产总额 1,915 亿元，较年初增长 413 亿元，增幅 22.78%；各项存款总额 1,507 亿元，较年初增长 286 亿元，增幅 23.44%；各项贷款总额 1,211 亿元，较年初增长 259 亿元，增幅 27.15%。全年实现净利润 6.39 亿元，整体经营成果好于预期。

一是持续提升公司治理水平。本行认真落实《银行保险机构公司治理准则》等监管要求，不断加强董事会履职能力建设，公司治理的有效性进一步提升。全面落实党委会研究审议的前置程序要求，有效突出党组织在公司治理结构中的领导核心地位。修订印发了《齐商银行全面风险管理政策》《齐商银行股权管理办法》等规章制度，公司治理主体的职责边界和决策程序更加清晰。制定执行了《〈健全银行业保险业公司治理三年行动方案（2020-2022年）〉实施意见》，借鉴良好实践和立足自身实际相结合，着力健全体制机制，公司治理的科学性、稳健性和有效性不断增强。组织开展了公司治理自查评估工作，总结了经验，明确了差距，为公司治理的持续完善提供了最新依据。持续提升股权管理水平，规范开展股东资格审查、股权变更、质押等日常工作，有效评估股东经营管理状况，严把股东准入关口。组织主要股东根据监管要求签订承诺声明，切实履行相关义务。依法合规开展关联交易管理，动态、全面地做好关联方认定，有效防范风险在本行、股东及其关联机构间传染和转移。坚持董事会全面工作部署、高级管理层贯彻落实并定期报告、监事会有效监督的运行机制，董事会战略决策、高级管理层执行落实、监事会监督制衡的协调运行机制更加高效。

二是扎实推进业务结构调整。本行坚持稳字当头、稳中求进，坚守“三服务”市场定位，树立“发展提质增效、管理创先争优”的导向，不断提升金融服务质效，各项业务实现了新的跨越。一是零售业务较快发展。贯彻“零售业务批发做”理念，坚定实施“大零售战略”。普惠金融占比提升，全面完成“两增两控”监管目标；消费金融持续拓展，年末消费贷款余额达到125.7亿元，较年初增长27.2%，信用卡增长量、活跃率均居山东城商行联盟成员行首位；储蓄存款增幅领先，年末余额达到586.5亿元，增量增幅均居全省城商行前列，储蓄存款占比达到38.9%。二是公司业务稳中有进。对公存款稳步提量，积极开展综合营销、联动营销、分层营销，年末对公存款余额达到920.45亿元，较年初增长22.28%；对公贷款结构优化，落实全市高水平技术改造财政支持政策，累计为161个技改项目投放“齐银技改贷”21.3亿元，撬动技改项目总投资234亿元，赢得了省市政府的充分认可和高度评价。三是科技赋能成效显著。大力实施“金融科技战略”。线上化产品研发不断提速，持续升级“场景贷”系列产品，年内发放各类线上贷款占全部普惠小微贷款投放量的22.2%，较年初提升11个百分点，借助“在线供应链金融平台”，拓宽了服务小微企业和城乡居民的新领域；多元化网络渠道不断拓展，手机银行6.0版正式上线运行，活跃率和交易额持续增长，网络交易替代率居省内城商行前列；场景化平台建设不断丰富，积极融入社会场景，通过网络技术实现居民就医、教育、生活缴费等无现金自助服务，改进银企直联平台，打造“齐商聚财”平台，为商户收单业务及后续开展数据化营销奠定了基础；标准化数据应用不断整合，研发智慧报表平台，提升数据治理及应用水平，充实数据来源，优化数据模型，为综合营销提供了数据支撑。

三是不断强化风险防控机制。本行坚持稳健审慎的风险管理理念，对各类风险实施重点监督和有效管控，保障了全行高质量发展。一是合规文化再上新台阶，扎实开展“规范建设提升年”和“内控合规管理建设年”活动，强化“十二项机制”建设，用好“案防阻击图”项目试点成果，完善了相关制度流程和管理机制，

有力提升了合规内控管理水平；持续完善问责体系，明确定责、追责环节及处罚标准，有效发挥了责任追究的警示惩戒作用。二是风险防控实现新提升，加强全面风险管理，集中上收授信审批权限，减少审批节点，优化审批模式，加强信审会建设，强化集中放款管理，提升了授信业务审批质效，为持续打造“履职尽责、尽职免责、严肃追责、全程覆盖”的良性信贷文化奠定了基础；突出抓好金融消费者权益保护，荣获“全省反洗钱知识网络竞赛”集体一等奖；稽核内审“第三道防线”作用持续发挥，实现了全年安保“零事故”“零案件”。三是不良资产处置取得新成效，借力监管推动和地方政府支持，突出抓好重点机构、重点大户清收工作，存量不良贷款清收处置取得实质进展，全面完成了风险攻坚战目标任务。

四是深入实施全方位改革。本行积极贯彻“以人为本”的发展理念，企业文化建设、人才队伍建设、精神文明建设成效显著。一是“齐银文化”内涵不断丰富，持续推动“标杆文化”“干事文化”“执行文化”等新元素的再总结、再提炼、再提升，以优秀先进文化引领全行高质量发展，“有内涵、有梦想、有特色、有温度、有定力”的企业愿景持续展现，“齐银文化”的内在凝聚力和外在影响力进一步增强。二是全方位改革扎实推进，人才团队持续优化，建成了千名人才组成的“三级人才库”，其中本科以上学历占比为98.1%，近两年从中提拔晋级使用近300人，其中本科以上学历占比为99.6%，平均年龄35岁，招聘引进各层次专业人才，为本行管理岗位及专业条线补充了“新鲜血液”，启动“上下交流机制”，完善“公开选聘机制”“内退转岗机制”和“‘人才库’动态调整机制”，人力资源管理机制逐步健全成熟；组织架构更加完善，规范定义总行部室职责和人员岗位设置，形成了更为科学的职责体系，优化分支行组织架构，提升了条线管理的系统性高效性；职级体系更加科学，明确了岗位编制，完善了职级序列体系，为干部员工职业规划和职级晋升提供了广阔舞台；激励机制更加有效，按照“倾斜前台、倾斜一线”的考核导向，梳理修订了各层面综合考核办法，有力彰显了激励引领作用，进一步调动了全员积极性创造性。三是社会责任践行更加有力。秉持具有社会责任感的职业规范和价值准则，坚持以职代会为基本形式的民主管理制度，规范程序、丰富内容、注重实效，充分保护员工合法权益；工会、团委积极发挥作用，持续为广大员工办实事、办好事，全员获得感幸福感安全感更加充实；积极争创省市级文明单位，实现了经营管理与精神文明建设齐发展、共提高；社区银行、客服中心、微信公众平台等载体功能进一步增强，文明规范服务水平不断提高，全行的社会形象和品牌影响力进一步提升。

2022 年，本行将全面贯彻党的十九大和十九届历次全会以及中央经济工作会议精神，坚持“稳字当头、稳中求进”的总基调，锚定“质量第一、效益优先”的工作目标，围绕“结构调整、转型发展”的工作主线，以高质量发展为主题，以改革创新为动力，以价值创造为导向，实施“普惠金融、三农金融、绿色金融、场景金融、产业金融、供应链金融”“六大工程”，推进“专业化、精细化、轻型化、平台化、数字化”“五化转型”，以高品质的金融服务、高素质的人才团队、高效率的管理机制、高质效的经营成果，推进本行高质量发展再上新台阶，实现发展“质量、结构、规模、速度、效益、安全”相统一，努力打造“特色鲜明、管理科学、内控严密、价值卓越”的现代化零售精品银行！

# Chairman's Speech

In 2021, Qishang Bank, under the correct leadership of CPC Committees, governments and regulatory authorities at all levels and the strong support of all sectors of the society, adhered to the general tone of seeking progress while maintaining stability, forged ahead and overcame difficulties, realized coordinated development of scale, quality and efficiency, and won recognition and praise from customers, shareholders and all sectors of the society. By the end of 2021, the total assets of the Bank had reached RMB 191.5 billion, an increase of 41.3 billion over the beginning of the year, with a growth rate of 22.78%; all deposits had totaled RMB 150.7 billion, an increase of RMB 28.6 billion over the beginning of the year, with a growth rate of 23.44%; and all loans had totaled RMB 121.1 billion, an increase of RMB 25.9 billion over the beginning of the year, with a growth rate of 27.15%. The annual net profit was RMB 639 million, and the overall operating results were better than expected.

First, the Bank continued to improve the level of corporate governance. The Bank conscientiously met regulatory requirements such as the Governance Code for Banking and Insurance Institutions and Corporations, constantly strengthened the ability of the Board of Directors to perform their duties, and further improved the effectiveness of corporate governance. We fully practiced the procedural requirements of the CPC Committee's research and deliberation, and effectively highlighted the leading and core position of the CPC organization in the corporate governance structure. Rules and regulations such as Comprehensive Risk Management Policy of Qishang Bank and Equity Management Measures of Qishang Bank were revised and issued, making the division of responsibilities and decision-making procedures of corporate governance subjects clearer. The implementation Rules on the Three-Year Action Plan for Improving Corporate Governance in the Banking and Insurance Industry (2020-2022) was formulated and implemented. The Bank learned from good practices, made great efforts to improve the system and mechanism based on its own reality, and constantly enhanced the scientific, robust and effective nature of corporate governance. The Bank organized the self-inspection and evaluation of corporate governance, summarized the experience, identified where the gap is, and provided the latest basis for the continuous improvement of corporate governance. The Bank continuously improved the level of equity management, standardized the daily affairs of shareholder qualification review, equity change, pledge, etc., effectively evaluated the operation and management of shareholders, and strictly controlled the access of shareholders. Major shareholders were organized to sign commitment statements according to regulatory requirements and earnestly perform relevant obligations. We managed related party transactions in accordance with laws and regulations, dynamically and comprehensively identified related parties, and effectively prevented risks from spreading and transferring among the Bank, shareholders and their affiliates. The Bank adhered to the operation mechanism of overall work deployment by the Board of Directors, implementation and regular reporting by senior management, and effective supervision by the Board of Supervisors. The coordination operation mechanism of strategic decision-making by the Board of Directors, implementation by senior management, and supervision by the Board of Supervisors was more efficient.

Second, the Bank made solid progress in adjusting the strategic structure. The Bank kept to the principle of "seeking progress while maintaining stability," established the guidance of "improving the quality and efficiency of development, and striving for excellence in management," constantly improved the quality and efficiency of financial services, and secured new leaps in a variety of businesses. The business structure had been continuously optimized, and the market positioning of "three services" had been followed, the structural adjustment had harvested remarkable results, the structural adjustment has achieved remarkable results by adhering to the "three services" market positioning. We put the concept of "wholesale-based operation of retail business," firmly implemented the "big retail strategy," lifted the proportion of inclusive finance, and comprehensively completed the regulatory goal of "two increases and two controls". Consumer finance continued to expand, and the balance of consumer loans at the end of the year reached RMB 12.57 billion, an increase of 27.2% over the beginning of the year; and the growth and activity rate of credit cards ranked first among the member banks of Shandong City Commercial Banks Alliance. The growth rate of savings deposits took the lead, with the year-end balance reaching RMB 58.65 billion, and the incremental quantity and growth rate was among the top of Shandong City Commercial Banks Alliance, accounting for 38.9% of savings deposits. We steadily increased the amount of corporate deposits, and positively carried out comprehensive marketing, linkage marketing and hierarchical marketing; optimized the structure of public loans, implemented the city's financial support policy for high-level technological transformation, invested RMB 2.13 billion in "QSBANK Technical Transformation Loan" for 161 technological transformation projects, and brought in a total investment of RMB 23.4 billion to technological transformation projects; and won the full recognition and high praises of provincial and municipal governments. Empowerment through scientific and technology had achieved significant results. The "financial technology strategy" was implemented, and the research and development of online products was accelerated. We continued to upgrade the "scene loan" series, and various online loans issued accounted for 22.2% of all inclusive small- and micro-sized loans in the year, an increase of 11% over the beginning of the year. The "online supply chain financial platform" had helped broaden the new field of serving small- and micro-sized enterprises and urban and rural residents. The diversified network channels had been expanded from time to time. Version 6.0 of the mobile bank application was officially launched, the activity rate and transaction volume continued to grow, and the substitution rate by online transactions was among the top urban commercial banks in Shandong province. The scene based platforms had been constantly constructed and enriched. We vigorously integrated the social scene, and presented cashless self-services covering medical treatment, education and living payment for residents, etc. through network technology. We improved the bank-enterprise direct connection platform and create the platform "Qishang Jucai", laying a foundation for merchant settlement business and subsequent digital marketing. Standardized data applications were integrated to a higher level. We researched and developed a smart reporting platform, improved data governance and application level, enriched data sources, optimized data models, and provided data support for comprehensive marketing.

Third, the Bank enhanced the risk prevention and control mechanism. The Bank followed the concept of prudent risk management, conducted key supervision and effective control over various risks, and ensured the high-quality development of the Bank. Compliance culture had grown to a new level. We organized the activities of "Year of Standardized Construction and Improvement" and "Year of Internal Control and Compliance Management Construction" practically, strengthened the construction of "Twelve Mechanisms", made good use of the pilot results of the "Case Prevention Diagram" project, completed the relevant system processes and management mechanisms, and effectively elevated the level of compliance and internal control management. We

improved the accountability system, made clear the responsibility determination, accountability links and punishment standards, and put the warning and punishment role of accountability into good use. Risk prevention and control had been promoted. The Bank strengthened comprehensive risk management, centralized collection of credit approval authority, reduced approval links, optimized the approval mode, enhanced the construction of the credit review committee, promoted centralized lending management, improved the quality and efficiency of credit business approval, and laid the foundation for continuing to build a benign credit culture of "performing duties with due diligence, exempting responsibilities for due diligence, serious accountability, and full coverage". We focused on the protection of the rights and interests of financial consumers, and won the first collective prize of the "Provincial Anti-money Laundering Knowledge Network Competition". "The third line of defense" of internal audit was put to good use, with "zero accidents" and "zero cases" of security throughout the year. New achievements were obtained in the disposal of non-performing assets. With regulatory authority promotion and local government support, we highlighted the loan repayment by key institutions and major households. Substantial progress was made in the repayment and disposal of non-performing loans in stock, and the risks were fully removed.

Fourth, the Bank deepened all-round reform. The Bank put the development concept of "people-oriented" into effect and achieved notable results in construction of corporate culture, talent team and spiritual civilization. The connotation of "QSBANK Culture" was further enriched. We vigorously practiced professional norms and values with a sense of social responsibility, continued to promote further summary, refinement and improvement of new elements such as "benchmark culture", "officer culture" and "execution culture", led the high-quality development of the Bank with excellent advanced culture, demonstrated the corporate vision of "connotation, dream, characteristics, temperature and determination", and enhanced the internal cohesion and external influence of "QSBANK Culture" to a greater extent. Solid progress was made in all-round reform. The talent team was further optimized, and a "three-level talent pool" composed of 1,000 talents was built, of which 98.1% had a bachelor's degree or above. Nearly 300 employees were promoted in the past two years, of which 99.6% had a bachelor's degree or above, and the average age was 35 years old. Professionals at all levels had been recruited to introduce "fresh blood" to the Bank's management posts and professional lines. The "up and down communication mechanism" was launched, and the "open recruitment mechanism", "early retirement and job transfer mechanism" and "dynamic adjustment mechanism of talent pool" were further completed. The human resource management mechanism was gradually improved and became mature. The organizational structure was more complete, the responsibilities of the Head Office departments and offices and the setting of personnel positions were standardized, and a more scientific responsibility system was formed. The organizational structure of branches and sub-branches was optimized, and the systematic and efficient management of business lines was promoted. The job level system was more scientific, which clarified the post establishment, improved the job level sequence system, and created a broad stage for the career planning and promotion of cadres and employees. The incentive mechanism was more effective. In view of the assessment guidance of "giving priority to the front desk and the front line", the comprehensive assessment methods at all levels had been combed and revised, which highlighted the role of incentive and guidance, and further aroused the enthusiasm and creativity of all staff. The rights and interests of stakeholders were better protected. The Bank embraced the democratic management system in the basic form of workers' congress, standardized its procedures, enriched its contents, laid emphasis on effectiveness, and fully protected the legitimate rights and interests of employees. The trade union and the Youth League Committee actively handled concrete and beneficial affairs for the staff, and the sense of achievement, happiness and security of all staff had become more substantial. We strove to create a civilized unit at the provincial and municipal levels, and realized the simultaneous development and improvement of business management and spiritual civilization construction. The functions of community banks, customer service centers, WeChat public platforms and other carriers were further enhanced, the level of civilized and standardized services was elevated, and the social image and brand influence of the Bank were brought to a higher level.

In 2022, the Bank will comprehensively practice the spirit of the 19th National Congress of the Communist Party of China, the previous plenary sessions of the 19th National Congress of the Communist Party of China and the Central Economic Work Conference, follow the general tone of "seeking progress while maintaining stability", keep the work objective of "quality first, benefit first" in mind, focus on the main line of "structural adjustment, transformation and development", and take high-quality development as the theme, reform and innovation as the driver, and value creation as the guidance. We will carry out inclusive finance, rural finance, green finance, scenario finance, industrial finance and supply chain finance, promote the transformation towards specialization, refinement, lightness, platform and digitalization; promote the high-quality development of the Bank to a new height with high-quality financial services and talent team, efficient management mechanism and effective business results; realize the coordinated development of "quality, structure, scale, speed, efficiency and safety"; and endeavor to create a modern retail boutique bank with "distinctive characteristics, scientific management, strict internal control and remarkable value"!

ice Party Secretary and President: Zhao Xiaodong

# 重要提示

齐商银行股份有限公司（以下简称“本公司”或“本行”）董事会、监事会及董事、监事和高级管理人员保证年度报告的内容真实、准确、完整，不存在虚假记载、误导性陈述或重大遗漏，并承担个别及连带责任。

本行第七届董事会第十四次会议于 2022 年 4 月 28 日召开，审议通过了本年度报告。会议应到董事 12 人，实到董事 11 人。

本年度财务会计报告已经由立信会计师事务所按照国家企业会计准则的规定进行了审计，经注册会计师张爱国、蔡文彬签字，出具了标准无保留意见的审计报告。

本行董事长李大鹏、行长赵晓东、财务部门负责人王新兵保证本年度报告中财务会计报告的真实、完整。

齐商银行股份有限公司董事会

## Important Notice

The Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors, directors, supervisors and senior executives of Qishang Bank Co., Ltd. (hereinafter referred to as “the Company” or “the Bank”) shall guarantee that this Annual Report is real, accurate and complete and doesn’t constitute any false record, misleading statement or major omission, and shall bear individual and joint liabilities.

This Annual Report was deliberated and adopted at the 14th meeting of the seventh Board of Directors of the Bank held on April 28, 2022. The meeting shall have 12 directors being present, and the actual number of the directors is 11.

The annual financial report has been audited by BDO China Shu Lun Pan Certified Public Accountants LLP in accordance with China’s Accounting Standard for Business Enterprises, and has been signed by the CPAs Zhang Aiguo and Cai Wenbin with standard unqualified audit report issued.

The Chairman Li Dapeng, the President Zhao Xiaodong, and the Head of the Financial Department Wang Xinbing hereby warrant the authenticity and integrity of the financial report in this Annual Report.

The Board of Directors of QISHANG BANK CO., LTD.

1. 业务规模稳步增长。截至报告期末，本行集团口径资产总额1,914.74亿元，较年初增加413.37亿元，增幅27.53%；母公司法人口径资产总额1,835.4亿元，较年初增加442.32亿元，增幅31.75%；集团口径贷款总额1,211.44亿元，较年初增加258.65亿元，增幅27.15%；母公司法人口径贷款总额1,136.65亿元，较年初增加249.1亿元，增幅28.07%。集团口径负债总额1,750.14亿元，较年初增加383.07亿元，增幅28.02%；母公司法人口径负债总额1,676.36亿元，较年初增加412.94亿元，增幅32.68%；集团口径存款总额1,506.97亿元，较年初增加286.17亿元，增幅23.44%；母公司法人口径存款总额1,405.3亿元，较年初增加304.77亿元，增幅27.69%。

2. 经营效益保持平稳。截至报告期末，集团口径实现归属母公司净利润6.39亿元，较同期增加0.78亿元，增幅13.95%；母公司法人口径实现净利润5.97亿元，较同期增加0.38亿元，增幅6.78%。集团口径成本收入比为43.04%，较同期下降3.45个百分点。其中，发生业务及管理费用13.86亿元，较同期上升13.67%；营业收入32.19亿元，较同期上升22.78%。母公司法人口径成本收入比为41.81%，较同期下降3.16个百分点。其中，发

生业务及管理费用 12.17 亿元，较同期上升 12.98%；营业收入 29.12 亿元，较同期上升 21.53%。

3. 业务结构更加优化。报告期内，本行坚守“三服务”市场定位，结构调整成效明显。一是大零售条线实现跨越发展。贯彻“零售业务批发做”理念，坚定实施“大零售战略”，普惠金融占比提升，全面完成“两增两控”监管目标，荣获人民银行“2021年山东省金融服务小微企业能力竞赛”二等奖。二是大公司条线实现优化发展。大力开展“有组织、上规模、高层次”的系列营销活动，对公存款稳步提量，围绕“增资格、增客户、增系统”，积极开展综合营销、联动营销、分层营销。对公贷款结构优化，累计为161个技改项目投放“齐银技改贷”21.3亿元，撬动技改项目总投资234亿元，赢得了省市政府的充分认可和高度评价。发放全市首笔“碳配额质押贷”，报告期末绿色信贷、制造业贷款、涉农贷款均较年初有所增长，信贷结构调整取得积极成效。

4. 规范管理更加精细。报告期内，本行推动全方位改革措施逐项落地，开辟了“二次创业、再攀高峰”的崭新局面。人才团队更加充实，建成千名人才组成的“三级人才库”，为全行管理岗位及专业条线补充了“新鲜血液”。启动“上下交流机制”，完善“公开选聘机制”，全行人力资源管理机制逐步健全成熟。职级体系更加科学，明确了岗位编制，完善了职级序列体系。激励机制更加有效，按照“倾斜前台、倾斜一线”的考核导向，梳理修订了各层面综合考核办法，进一步调动了全员积极性创造性。

5. 创新成效更加显著。大力实施“金融科技战略”，抓好系统平台建设，提升客户体验及服务水平，强化科技赋能业务发展。一是线上化产品研发不断提速。持续升级“场景贷”系列产品，报告期内发放各类线上贷款占全部普惠小微贷款投放量的22.2%，较年初提升11个百分点。二是多元化网络渠道不断拓展，网络交易替代率居省内城商行前列。三是场景化平台建设不断丰富。积极融入社会场景，通过网络技术实现居民就医、教育、生活缴费等无现金自助服务。打造“齐商聚财”平台，为商户收单业务及后续开展数据化营销提供了有力支撑。四是标准化数据应用不断整合，充实数据来源，优化数据模型，为综合营销提供了数据支撑。

6. 风险防控科学有效。报告期内，全面风险管理暨新资本管理办法实施项目稳步推进，风险精细化管理水平不断提升。一是持续完善全面风险管理架构，通过健全全面风险管理政策体系，明确“三道防线”和各级管理部门责任分工，确保信用风险、市场风险、操作风险、流动性风险、声誉风险、信息科技风险等重要风险得到全程、全面防范控制。二是持续完善本行风险偏好、风险限额管理体系，根据监管要求，定期修订完善风险偏好、风险限额，优化风险偏好量化指标值，定期组织风险监测分析，对出现接近预警值和风险限额的业务及时做出风险预警，做到早发现、早预警、早处置。三是建立全面风险管理评估机制。定期针对本行面临的主要风险，从内在风险水平、风险管理能力、风险发展趋势，对单项风险总体状况进行了客观风险评估，形成风险评估报告，为下一步风险管控提供了管理依据和管控重点。四是多措并举，加强重点风险防控工作。强化风险指标监测及限额管理，及时做好风险预警，有效防控市场风险、信息科技风险。通过运用“内控合规及操作风险管理系统”，丰富管理手段，细化指标“颗粒度”，加强柜面实体印章及客户印鉴卡管理，有效管控操作风险。坚持流动性优先原则，科学调度配置资产负债结构，保障备付率水平，有效防控流动性风险。强化舆情监测，拓展宣传渠道，提升社会形象，加大舆情事件处置力度，有效管控声誉风险。截至报告期末，本行集团口径五级分类不良贷款率1.67%，拨备覆盖率141.35%，拨贷比2.36%。

## 二、主要业务运作情况

1. 个人存款情况：截至报告期末，本行集团口径本外币个人存款余额586.52亿元，比年初增加118.43亿元，增幅25.3%，在各项存款中占比38.92%，比年初上升0.58个百分点。

2. 个人贷款情况：截至报告期末，本行集团口径本外币个人贷款余额 211.99 亿元，比年初增加 48.71 亿元，增幅 29.83%，在各项贷款中占比 17.5%，比年初上升 0.36 个百分点。

3. 对公存款情况：截至报告期末，本行集团口径本外币对公存款余额 920.45 亿元，比年初增加 167.74 亿元，

增幅 22.28%，在各项存款中占比 61.08%，比年初下降 0.58 个百分点。

4. 对公贷款情况：截至报告期末，本行集团口径本外币对公贷款余额 999.45 亿元，比年初增加 209.93 亿元，增幅 26.59%，在各项贷款中占比 82.5%，比年初下降 0.36 个百分点。

1. The business grew steadily. As of the end of the reporting period, the total group assets of the Bank was RMB 191.474 billion, with an increase of RMB 41.337 billion or 27.53% over the beginning of the year; the total assets of the parent company was RMB 183.54 billion, with an increase of RMB 44.232 billion or 31.75% over the beginning of the year; the total amount of group loans was RMB 121.144 billion, with an increase of RMB 25.865 billion or 27.15% over the beginning of the year; and the total amount of corporate loans of the parent company was RMB 113.665 billion, with an increase of RMB 24.91 billion or 28.07% over the beginning of the year. The total liabilities was RMB 175.014 billion, with an increase of RMB 38.307 billion or 28.02% over the beginning of the year; the total liabilities of the parent company was RMB 167.636 billion, with an increase of RMB 41.294 billion or 32.68% over the beginning of the year; the total deposits was RMB 150.697 billion, with an increase of RMB 28.617 billion or 23.44% over the beginning of the year; and the total amount of corporate deposits of the parent company was RMB 140.53 billion, with an increase of RMB 30.477 billion or 27.69% over the beginning of the year.

2.The operating benefits remained stable. As of the end of the reporting period, the net profit attributable to the parent company

of RMB 639 million, with an increase of RMB 78 million or 13.95% over the last year; and the net profit of the parent company was RMB 597 million, with an increase of RMB 38 million or 6.78% over the last year. The cost-income ratio was 43.04%, with a year-on-year decrease of 3.45%. Among them, the business and management expenses was RMB 1.386 billion, with an increase of 13.67% over the last year; the operating income was RMB 3.219 billion, with an increase of 22.78% over the last year. The cost-income ratio of the parent company was 41.81%, with a year-on-year decrease of 3.16%. Among them, the business and management expenses was RMB 1.217 billion, with an increase of 12.98% over the last year; the operating income was RMB 2.912 billion, with an increase of 21.53% over the last year.

3. The business structure is more optimized. During the reporting period, the Bank adhered to the market positioning of “three services” and achieved remarkable results in structural adjustment. First, large retail lines have achieved leapfrog development. The Bank implemented the concept of “wholesale retail business”, firmly implemented the “big retail strategy”, increased the proportion of inclusive financing, fully achieved the regulatory objectives of “two increases and two controls”, and won the second prize of “2021 Shandong Financial Services Small and Micro Enterprise Ability Competition” by the People’s Bank of China. Second, the lines of large companies have achieved optimized development. The Bank vigorously carried out a series of “organized, large-scale and high-level” marketing activities, steadily increased the amount of public deposits, and actively carried out comprehensive marketing, linkage marketing and hierarchical marketing around “increasing qualifications, customers and systems”. The Bank optimized the structure of corporate loans, and invested RMB 2.13 billion in “QS BANK Technical Transformation Loan” for 161 technical transformation projects, inciting a total investment of RMB 23.4 billion in technical transformation projects, which won full recognition and high praise from provincial and municipal governments. The Bank issued the city’s first “carbon emission quota pledge loan”. At the end of the reporting period, green credit, manufacturing loans and agriculture-related loans all increased compared with the beginning of the year, and the credit structure adjustment achieved positive results.

4. Standardized management is more detailed. During the reporting period, the Bank promoted the implementation of all-round reform measures item by item, opening up a brand-new situation of “starting a new business and climbing the peak again”. The talent team has been enriched, and a “three-level talent pool” composed of thousands of talents has been built, which has added “fresh blood” to the management positions and professional lines of the whole bank. The Bank has started the “communication mechanism between superiors and subordinates”, and improved the “open selection mechanism”, and is gradually improving and perfecting the human resources management mechanism of the whole bank. The rank system is more scientific, the post establishment is clear, and the rank sequence system is improved. The incentive mechanism is more effective. According to the assessment orientation of “focus on the front desk and the front line”, the comprehensive assessment methods at all levels have been combed and revised, further mobilizing the enthusiasm and creativity of all employees.

5.The innovation effect is more remarkable. The Bank vigorously implemented the Financial Technology Strategy, paid special attention to the construction of system platform, improved customer experience and service level, and strengthened the development of technology empowerment business. First, the research and development of online products is accelerating. The Bank continuously upgraded its "Scenario Loan" series products, and various online loans issued during the reporting period accounted for 22.2% of all inclusive small and micro loans, with an increase of 11% over the beginning of the year. Second, diversified network channels are constantly expanding, and the replacement rate of online transactions ranks among the top city commercial banks in the province. Third, the construction of scene platform is constantly enriched. By actively integrating into the social scene, the Bank realizes cashless self-service such as residents' medical treatment, education and living expenses through network technology. By creating a platform of "gathering wealth from QSBANK", the Bank provides strong support for merchants' acquiring business and subsequent data marketing. Fourth, standardized data applications are continuously integrated, enriching data sources and optimizing data models, providing data support for comprehensive marketing.

6. Risk prevention and control is scientific and effective. During the reporting period, the implementation project of comprehensive risk management and new capital management measures was steadily advanced, and the level of refined risk management was continuously improved. First, the Bank continuously improved the comprehensive risk management structure, and ensured that important risks such as credit risk, market risk, operational risk, liquidity risk, reputational risk and information technology risk were prevented and controlled in the whole process by improving the comprehensive risk management policy system and clarifying the "three lines of defense" and the division of responsibilities among management departments at all levels. Second, the Bank continuously improved the Bank's risk preference and risk limit management system, regularly revised and improved the risk preference and risk limit according to regulatory requirements, optimized the quantitative index value of risk preference, regularly organized risk monitoring and analysis, and made timely risk warning for businesses close to the warning value and risk limit, so as to achieve early detection, early warning and early disposal. Third, the Bank established a comprehensive risk management evaluation mechanism. According to the major risks faced, the Bank regularly conducted objective risk assessment on the overall situation of individual risks from the internal risk level, risk management ability and risk development trend, and formed a risk assessment report, which provided management basis and control focus for the next step of risk management and control. Fourth, the Bank took multiple measures to strengthen the prevention and control of key risks. The Bank strengthened risk indicator monitoring and quota management, made timely risk early warning, and effectively prevented and controlled market risks and information technology risks. By using the "Internal Control Compliance and Operational Risk Management System", the Bank enriched management means, refined the indicator "granularity", strengthened the management of counter physical seals and customer seal cards, and effectively controlled operational risks. The Bank adhered to the principle of liquidity priority, scientifically dispatched and allocated asset-liability structure, guaranteed the level of cash reserve ratio, and effectively prevented and controlled liquidity risks. The Bank strengthened public opinion monitoring, expanded publicity channels, enhanced social image, intensified the handling of public opinion incidents, and effectively controlled reputational risks. As of the end of the reporting period, the non-performing loan ratio of the Bank's five-tier group classification was 1.67%, the provision coverage ratio was 141.35%, and the appropriation-to-loan ratio was 2.36%.

## II. Operation Situation of Main Business

1. Personal deposits: As of the end of the reporting period, the balance of personal deposits in local and foreign currencies of the Bank was RMB 58.652 billion, with an increase of RMB 11.843 billion or 25.3% over the beginning of the year, accounting for 38.92% of all deposits, with an increase of 0.58% over the beginning of the year.

2. Personal loans: As of the end of the reporting period, the balance of personal loans in local and foreign currencies of the Bank was RMB 21.199 billion, with an increase of RMB 4.871 billion or 29.83% over the beginning of the year, accounting for 17.5% of all loans, with an increase of 0.36% over the beginning of the year.

3. Corporate deposits: As of the end of the reporting period, the balance of corporate deposits in local and foreign currencies of the Bank was RMB 92.045 billion, with an increase of RMB 16.774 billion or 22.28% over the beginning of the year, accounting for 61.08% of all deposits, with a decrease of 0.58% from the beginning of the year.

4. Corporate loans: As of the end of the reporting period, the balance of corporate loans in local and foreign currencies of the Bank was RMB 99.945 billion, with an increase of RMB 20.993 billion or 26.59% over the beginning of the year, accounting for 82.5% of all loans, with a decrease of 0.36% from the beginning of the year.

### 2. 股权变更情况

报告期内，本行股东淄博齐信产业投资有限公司、淄博鑫能能源集团有限公司进行股份转受让。截至报告期末，淄博齐信产业投资有限公司持有本行19,742.43万股，持股比例4.98%，淄博鑫能能源集团有限公司持有本行8,985万股，持股比例为2.27%。

## (三) 股本变动情况

报告期内，本行召开2020年度股东大会，审议通过了《关于2020年度利润分配方案的议案》，总股本由3,887,921,566股增至3,965,679,997股。

## 二、年内召开股东大会情况

2021 年 5 月 25 日，本行 2020 年度股东大会在总行二楼群英厅召开，出席本次会议的股东和股东代表 31 名，代表有表决权股份数 277,907 万股，占总股本的 71%，符合《中华人民共和国公司法》和本行《公司章程》的有关规定。董事长李大鹏先生主持了会议。会议经山东鲁杰律师事务所律师现场见证。会议经过有效表决，一致通

过如下决议：

1. 审议通过了《第七届董事会 2020 年度工作报告》；

2. 审议通过了《第七届监事会 2020 年度工作报告》；

3. 审议通过了《关于 2020 年度财务计划执行情况和 2021 年度财务预算草案的报告》；

4. 审议通过了《关于 2020 年度利润分配方案的议案》；

5. 审议通过了《关于制定 2021 年增资扩股方案的议案》；

6. 审议通过了《关于变更注册资本的议案》；

7. 审议通过了《关于修订〈齐商银行股份有限公司章程〉的议案》；

8. 审议通过了《关于选举董事的议案》；

9. 审议通过了《监事会关于 2020 年度董事及高级管理层成员履职评价情况的报告》；

10. 审议通过了《监事会关于 2020 年度监事履职评价情况的报告》。

大会还通报了《2020 年度前十大股东持股情况》《2020 年度关联方交易情况》《2020 年度前十大授信集团客户情况》。

## 三、董事会的构成及工作情况

## （二）董事会下设专门委员会

董事会下设战略规划委员会、关联交易控制委员会、风险管理委员会、薪酬考核及提名委员会、审计委员会、执行委员会、消费者权益保护委员会，各委员会议事规则健全，报告期内共召开33次会议，各位委员出席率符合监管要求，充分有效地支持了董事会发挥决策职能。

## （三）董事会工作情况

报告期内，董事会认真研究执行国家宏观经济政策，严格贯彻落实监管部门要求，不断加强董事会履职能力建设，本行经营管理和创新发展跃上了新台阶。一是充分保障股东权益，股东大会职能得到有效发挥。二是持续提升履职能力和决策水平，严格按照《公司章程》及议事规则，全面落实党委会研究审议的前置程序要求，有效突出党组织在公司治理结构中的领导核心地位。报告期内组织召开了四次例会、八次临时会议，对董事会职权范围内的业务报告和议案进行了讨论和审议，谨慎、认真、勤勉地履行了决策职责；修订印发了《齐商银行全面风险管理政策》等规章制度，公司治理主体的职责边界和决策程序更加清晰，治理体系更加协调完善。制定实施了《<健全银行业保险业公司治理三年行动方案（2020-2022年）>实施意见》，借鉴良好实践和立足自身实际相结合，着力健全体制机制，公司治理的科学性、稳健性和有效性不断增强。有效提升股权管理水平，持续坚持“长期稳定、透明诚信、公平合理”原则，主动防范有关风险。三是扎实推进战略转型和结构调整，经营业绩实现稳步增长。四是不断强化全面风险管理，风险管理及内控机制建设得到进一步完善。五是持续坚持创新引领，核心竞争力得到有效提升。六是不断深化资本管理，严格落实《齐商银行股份有限公司2019-2021年资本管理规划》，有效加强全行资本管理，满足业务发展和风险管理需要；持续拓宽外源性资本补充渠道，成功发行30亿元无固定期限资本债券，进一步壮大了资本实力，提高了服务实体经济能力和防范化解金融风险水平。七是不断规范信息披露渠道，对外信息披露透明度及规范性持续提高。

报告期内，两位独立董事依法按时参加了董事会会议，能够充分发挥从事金融、法律专业研究的特长，及时分析研读财务报表，关注公司经营管理状况，适时针对经营风险控制、薪酬分配政策、计划财务管理、董事会决议执行等法定关注事项，发表独立性意见，提出改进建议，履行独立监督职责。

## （二）监事会下设专门委员会

监事会下设监督委员会、提名委员会，各委员会议事规则健全，报告期内共召开10次会议，各位委员出席率符合监管要求，充分有效地发挥了监事会综合监督的职能。

## （三）监事会工作情况

报告期内，监事会认真履行本行章程和股东大会赋予的职权，充分发挥监事会的监督职能，协调运用多种监督手段，按照依法合规、客观公正、科学有效的原则，积极开展履职监督、财务监督、内控监督、风险管理监督，监督理念不断深化，监督能力不断提高，有效保护了本行、股东、职工、债权人和其他利益相关者合法权益。一是规范会议制度，履职有效性持续增强。组织召开了四次监事会例会和一次临时会议，研究审议了全面风险管理、内控合规管理、消费者权益保护、洗钱和恐怖融资风险管理、流动性风险管理、案件防控等50项议案及报告。按时召开监事会监督委员会、提名委员会例会，研究审议有关应议事项。组织监事列席董事会例会，听取议案及报告，并对法定监督议案或报告发表了监督意见；出席股东大会，并在股东大会上作监事会工作报告，保证了广大股东的合法权益。二是发挥专业优势，履职能力持续提升。积极参与董事会、高级管理层的经营管理活动，并对重大事项、重大决策进行有效监督；外部监事结合自身的专业知识、从业经历和工作经验，通过对行内干部员工开展经济金融政策形势专题讲座等形式充分履行职责，积极建言献策，支持全行转型发展；职工监事有效发挥日常直接参与行内经营管理等便利条件，全面参加历次专项监督工作，从本行的长远利益出发，推动了监事会更好地开展工作。三是突出工作重点，监督方式更加丰富。建立专项监督机制，通过分支机构调研、企业座谈、调阅资料和会议交流等形式，先后对声誉风险管控、普惠金融发展、授信审批改革、绩效考核政策实施等工作开展了专项监督，与董事会和高级管理层面对面沟通反馈监督意见，科学评估改革政策的落实成效。建立日常监督机制，按月或按季调取经营报表及条线关于内部控制、风险管理等原始资料，及时了解经营管理和风险状况，扩大监督工作的覆盖领域，从提升经营质效、加强不良资产管控、提高信贷精细化管理、加大稽核审计成果利用等方面提出了监督意见。四是立足职责定位，确保履职独立性。完善规章制度、健全法律文书，对监督意见反馈和落实情况实时跟踪，构建了与董事会、高级管理层的工作沟通渠道。严格遵守法律法规和本行章程，坚持原则、忠实履职，不存在超越职权妨碍或干预董事会及高级管理层正常工作及其他董事、高级管理人员履职活动的违规行为。

<div style="text-align: center;">(四) 外部监事工作情况</div>

报告期内，三位外部监事依法合规地参加了监事会会议，能够充分发挥从事金融专业研究的特长，及时分析研读财务报表，关注本行经营管理状况，适时针对本行战略创新、结构调整、经营风险控制、计划财务管理、监事会决议执行等法定关注事项，发表独立性意见，提出改进建议，履行独立监督职责。

## 五、高级管理层人员构成及基本情况

（一）本行总行高级管理层人员共有12人。其中：董事长1人，行长1人，副行长3人，行长助理2人，董事会秘书1人，内审负责人1人，财务负责人1人，首席信息官1人，总监1人。

（二）分支行主要管理人员共有156人，其中：分行（含淄博支行、鲁中支行）行长10人，分行（含淄博支行、鲁中支行）副行长、行长助理34人；管辖支行行长41人，管辖支行副行长、行长助理71人。

上述人员均符合监管部门规定的任职资格条件，并按要求通过了监管部门的任职资格核准或备案。

## 六、部门和分支机构设置情况

本行董事会下设战略规划委员会、关联交易控制委员会、风险管理委员会、薪酬考核及提名委员会、审计委员会、执行委员会、消费者权益保护委员会。监事会下设提名委员会、监督委员会。总行下设26个部室及西安分行等120家分支行。

## 七、年度薪酬情况

2021 年度本行董事会、监事会及高级管理层人员履职情况良好，有效完成全年任务目标。本行董事、监事和高级管理人员在本行领取薪酬的共 31 人，税前总计 1,227 万元。其中：本行高级管理人员 2021 年度发放薪酬税前共计 836 万元（含市管干部 2020 年度薪酬补差和 2017-2018 年度任期激励的 50%），包括年度绩效薪酬延期支付 57 万元，根据公司绩效薪酬延期支付和追索扣回管理办法，该延期支付延后三年考核发放。

本行高级管理人员的薪酬依据考核办法，按照季度考核结果核算兑现，其中市管干部薪酬按照市属国有企业负责人薪酬政策执行。独立董事、非执行董事、外部监事、股东监事的津贴根据尽职考核情况兑现。

#### 2. Changes in equity

During the reporting period, the Bank’s shareholders Zibo Qixin Industrial Investment Co., Ltd. and Zibo Xinneng Energy Group Co., Ltd. transferred their shares. As of the end of the reporting period, Zibo Qixin Industrial Investment Co., Ltd. held 197.4243 million shares of the Bank, accounting for 4.98%, and Zibo Xinneng Energy Group Co., Ltd. held 89.85 million shares of the Bank, accounting for 2.27%.

#### (III) Changes in share capital

During the reporting period, the Bank held the 2020 Annual General Meeting of Shareholders, reviewed and approved the Proposal on Profit Distribution Plan for 2020, and the total share capital increased from 3,887,921,566 shares to 3,965,679,997 shares.

## II. Situation of General Meetings of Shareholders Held in the Year

On May 25, 2021, the 2020 Annual General Meeting of Shareholders of the Bank was held in Qunying Hall on the second floor of the Head Office. Thirty-one shareholders and shareholders' representatives attended the meeting, representing 2.77907 billion voting shares, accounting for 71% of the total share capital, which complied with the relevant provisions of the Company Law of the People's Republic of China and the Articles of Association of the Bank. Chairman Li Dapeng presided over the meeting. The meeting was

witnessed by lawyers of Shandong Lujie Law Firm. The meeting unanimously adopted the following resolutions after effective voting:

1. Deliberated and adopted the 2020 Annual Work Report of the Seventh Board of Directors;

2. Deliberated and adopted the 2020 Annual Work Report of the Seventh Board of Supervisors;

3. Deliberated and adopted the Report on the Implementation of 2020 Annual Financial Plan and 2021 Annual Financial Budget Draft;

4. Deliberated and adopted the Proposal on the Profit Distribution Plan in 2020;

5. Deliberated and adopted the Proposal on Profit Distribution Plan in 2021;

6. Deliberated and adopted the Proposal on the Change of Registered Capital;

7. Deliberated and adopted the Proposal on Amending the <Articles of Association of QISHANG BANK CO., LTD.>;

8. Deliberated and adopted the Proposal on the Election of Directors;

9. Deliberated and adopted the Report of the Board of Supervisors on Performance Evaluation of Directors and Senior Executives in 2020;

10. Deliberated and adopted the Report of the Board of Supervisors on Performance Evaluation of Supervisors in 2020.

The meeting also announced 2020 Annual Shareholdings of Top Ten Shareholders, 2020 Annual Transactions of Affiliated Parties, and 2020 Annual Top Ten Credit Group Customers.

#### (I) Members of the Board of Directors

The Seventh Board of Directors of the Bank consists of 12 directors, including 1 chairman, 3 executive directors, 2 independent directors, 4 non-executive directors and 2 employee directors.

#### (II) Special committees under the Board of Directors

The Board of Directors has set up the Strategic Planning Committee, Related Transaction Control Committee, Risk Management Committee, Remuneration Assessment & Nomination Committee, Auditing Committee, Executive Committee and Consumer Rights Protection Committee with the sound rules of procedure. A total of 33 meetings were convened during the reporting period. The attendance rate of various committee members met the regulatory requirements, which supported the Board of Directors to play its role of decision-making fully and effectively.

##### (III) Work of the Board of Directors

During the reporting period, the Board of Directors researched and implemented the national macroeconomic policy earnestly, carried out the regulatory requirements of supervision departments strictly, and strengthened the performance capacity building of the Board of Directors constantly. The Bank reached a new stage in business management and innovative development. First, the Bank fully ensured the shareholders' equity and enabled the functions of the shareholders' meetings to play an effective role. Second, the Bank continued to improve its ability to perform its duties and decision-making level, fully implemented the requirements of pre-procedures studied and deliberated by CPC Committee, and effectively highlighted the core leadership position of the Party organization in the corporate governance structure, strictly in accordance with the Articles of Association and rules of procedure. During the reporting period, four regular meetings and eight interim meetings were organized to discuss and review the business reports and proposals within the scope of authority of the Board of Directors, and the decision-making duties were performed cautiously, conscientiously and diligently. The Comprehensive Risk Management Policy of QISHANG BANK and other rules and regulations were revised and issued, which made the responsibility boundaries and decision-making procedures of corporate governance subjects clearer and the governance system more coordinated and perfect. The Bank formulated and implemented the Implementation Opinions on the <Three-year Action Plan for Improving Corporate Governance in Banking and Insurance Industry (2020-2022)>, and made efforts to improve the system and mechanism by combining good practices with its own reality, thus continuously enhancing the scientificity, stability and effectiveness of corporate governance. The Bank effectively improved the level of equity management, continued to adhere to the principles of "long-term stability, transparency, integrity, fairness and rationality", and took the initiative to prevent relevant risks. Third, the Bank solidly pushed forward the strategic transformation and structural adjustment, so as to achieve the steady growth of operating results. Fourth, the Bank constantly strengthened the comprehensive risk management and further improved the construction of risk management and the internal control mechanism. Fifth, the Bank constantly insisted on innovation-driven development to effectively improve its core competitiveness. Sixth, the Bank continuously deepened its capital management, strictly implemented the Capital Management Plan of Sixth, the Bank continuously deepened its capital management, strictly implemented the Capital Management Plan of Qishang Bank Co., Ltd. from 2019 to 2021, and effectively strengthened the capital management of the whole bank, to meet the needs of business development and risk management; continued to broaden the channels for supplementing exogenous capital, and successfully issued RMB 3 billion of capital bonds with no fixed term, which further strengthened the capital strength, improved the ability to serve the real economy and prevented and resolved financial risks. Seventh, the Bank constantly standardized the information disclosure channel to improve the transparency and standardization of external information disclosure.

#### (II) Special committees under the Board of Supervisors

The Board of Supervisors set up the Supervision Committee and the Nomination Committee with sound rules of procedure. 10 meetings were convened during the reporting period. The attendance rate of the members was in line with the regulatory requirements, and the comprehensive supervision function of the Board of Supervisors was fully and effectively played.

#### (III) Work of the Board of Supervisors

During the reporting period, the Board of Supervisors earnestly performed the functions and powers entrusted by the Articles of Association of the Bank and the shareholders' meeting, gave full play to the supervisory function of the Board of Supervisors,

Coordinated the use of various supervision means, and actively carried out performance supervision, financial supervision, internal control supervision and risk management supervision in accordance with the principles of legal compliance, objectivity, fairness, science and effectiveness. The supervision concept has been continuously deepened and the supervision ability has been continuously improved, to effectively protect the legitimate rights and interests of the Bank, shareholders, employees, creditors and other stakeholders. First, the Board of Supervisors standardized the meeting system, and continuously enhanced the effectiveness of performing duties. The Board of Supervisors organized four regular meetings and one interim meeting of the Board of Supervisors, and studied and reviewed 50 proposals and reports including comprehensive risk management, internal control and compliance management, consumer rights protection, money laundering and terrorist financing risk management, liquidity risk management, and case prevention and control. The Board of Supervisors held regular meetings of the Supervisory Committee and Nomination Committee of the Board of Supervisors on time to study and consider relevant items to be discussed. The Board of Supervisors organized supervisors to attend regular meetings of the Board of Directors, listened to proposals and reports, and expressed supervision opinions on statutory supervision proposals or reports; and attended the shareholders' meeting and gave the work report of the Board of Supervisors at the shareholders' meeting, which ensured the legitimate rights and interests of the majority of shareholders. Second, the Board of Supervisors gave full play to professional advantages and continuously improved the ability to perform duties. The Board of Supervisors actively participated in the operation and management activities of the Board of Directors and senior management, and effectively supervised major issues and major decisions; external supervisors fully performed their duties by giving special lectures on economic and financial policy situation to cadres and employees in the Bank in combination with their own professional knowledge, working experience and work experience, and actively made suggestions to support the transformation and development of the whole bank; and employee supervisors gave full play to the convenience of daily direct participation in the operation and management of the Bank, and fully participated in all previous special supervision work, which promoted the Board of Supervisors to carry out its work better from the long-term interests of the Bank. Third, the Board of Supervisors highlighted the key points of work and enriched the supervision methods. The Board of Supervisors established a special supervision mechanism, carried out special supervision on reputational risk management and control, inclusive financing development, credit approval reform, performance appraisal policy implementation, etc. through branch investigation, enterprise discussion, access to materials and meeting exchange, had face-to-face communication with the Board of Directors and senior management to feed back supervision opinions, and scientifically evaluated the implementation effect of reform policies. The Board of Supervisors established a daily supervision mechanism, obtained the operating statements and the original data on internal control and risk management on a monthly or quarterly basis, timely understood the operating management and risk status, expanded the coverage of supervision work, and put forward supervision opinions from the aspects of improving operating quality and efficiency, strengthening the control of non-performing assets, improving the fine management of credit, and increasing the utilization of audit results. Fourth, the Board of Supervisors was based on its responsibilities to ensure its independence in performing its duties. The Board of Supervisors improved rules and regulations, improved legal documents, tracked the feedback and implementation of supervision opinions in real time, and built a working communication channel with the Board of Directors and senior management. The Board of Supervisors strictly abided by laws and regulations and the Articles of Association of the Bank, adhered to principles and faithfully performed its duties, and had no irregularities that hindered or interfered with the normal work of the Board of Directors and senior management and the performance of duties by other directors and senior executives.

During the reporting period, three External Supervisors attended the meetings of the Board of Supervisors in compliance with laws and regulations, gave full play to their expertise in financial research, analyzed and read financial statements in a timely manner, paid attention to the Bank’s management status, expressed independent opinions and brought forward improvement suggestions for the strategic innovation, structural adjustment, operational risk control, planned financial management, the execution of the resolutions of the Board of Supervisors, etc. in due course, and performed their independent supervision duties.

## V. Composition and Basic Information of Senior Management

(I) There are 12 senior management personnel in the head office of the Bank. Including 1 chairman, 1 president, 3 vice presidents, 2 assistants to the president, 1 secretary of the Board of Directors, 1 person in charge of internal audit, 1 person in charge of finance, 1 chief information officer and 1 director.

(II) There are 15 main management personnel in the brancnes and sub-brancnes, including 10 presidents of the brancnes (including Zibo Sub-branch and Luzhong Sub-branch) and 34 vice presidents and assistants to the president of the branches (including Zibo Sub-branch and Luzhong Sub-branch); and there are 41 presidents of sub-branches under jurisdiction, and 71 vice presidents and assistants to presidents of sub-branches under jurisdiction.

The above personnel are all in line with the qualifications stipulated by regulatory authorities, and have passed the qualification examination or filing of regulatory authorities as required.

## VI. Setup of Departments and Branch Organizations

The Board of Directors of the Bank has set up the Strategic Planning Committee, Related Transaction Control Committee, Risk Management Committee, Remuneration Assessment & Nomination Committee, Auditing Committee, Executive Committee and Consumer Rights Protection Committee. The Board of Supervisors has set up the Nomination Committee and Supervision Committee. The head office has 26 departments and offices and 120 sub-branches including Xi'an Branch.

## VII. Annual Remunerations

In 2021, the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors and the senior management of the Bank performed their duties well and effectively completed the annual mission objectives. A total of 31 directors, supervisors and senior executives of the Bank received remuneration from the Bank, totaling RMB 12.27 million before tax. Among them, the pre-tax salary paid to the senior executives of the Bank in 2021 totaled RMB 8.36 million (including 50% of the salary difference of municipal management cadres in 2020 and the term incentive in 2017-2018), including the deferred payment of annual performance salary of RMB 570,000. According to the Company's management measures for deferred payment and recourse deduction of performance salary, the deferred payment was postponed for three years.

The remuneration of the Bank’s senior executives was calculated and cashed according to the assessment methods and quarterly assessment results, and the remuneration of municipal management cadres was implemented according to the remuneration policy of the heads of municipal state-owned enterprises. The allowances of independent directors, non-executive directors, external supervisors and shareholder supervisors shall be paid according to due diligence assessment.

## 第四部分·各类风险和风险管理策略

## 一、风险种类及形式

本行在经营中主要面临以下风险：信用风险、市场风险、操作风险、流动性风险等。本行积极采取各种行之有效的措施，努力防范和化解上述风险。

## 二、风险管理策略

本行董事会及其下设风险管理委员会，高级管理层及其下设风险管理委员会，负责本行风险管理及控制。通过持续统筹推进全面风险管理体系项目建设，在各类风险管理和内部控制方面建立了符合现代银行监管要求的框架体系。各部门根据职责分工具体负责本行信用风险、市场风险、操作风险、流动性风险等风险的管理和控制。

## (一) 信用风险管理对策

本行严格执行信用风险相关制度流程，对信用风险实行全流程风险管控。按照年度授信指导意见，加大服务实体力度，信贷投向更趋科学合理，大力推进信贷结构调整优化，集中一切优势资源，支持普惠小微、先进制造业、绿色信贷等领域发展，同时严控房地产贷款、大额贷款投放速度，信贷业务转型发展效果明显。通过集中上收授信审批权限，减少审批节点，优化审批模式，多措并举强化授信风险防控，风险源头和重点领域风险管控更加严密。不良贷款处置工作扎实推进，授信业务实现科学稳健、持续向好发展。本行将大额风险暴露管理纳入全面风险管理体系，计量并动态监测大额风险暴露变动，有效管控客户集中度风险。截至报告期末，本行符合大额风险暴露标准的非同业单一客户、非同业关联客户、同业单一客户、同业集团客户均符合监管要求。

## （二）流动性风险管理对策

本行持续保持稳健的流动性风险管理策略，重视流动性指标的合规管理，实行重点流动性指标月度管理机制，提前规划业务总量和结构，预测、分析对流动性指标的影响，主动调整业务策略。同时不断提高资产负债管理能力，拓展资金来源渠道，确保负债总量适度、来源稳定、结构多元，提前做好备付准备和融资安排，增加优质流动性资产储备，加固应对外部冲击的“安全垫”。2021年流动性风险指标总体控制在合理范围内，主要流动性监管指标符合要求。

## （三）市场风险管理对策

本行持续优化市场风险监测报告机制，通过派驻风险经理定期开展业务风险分析，加强市场风险中台控制，持续加强对金融市场等相关业务的风险监测。根据市场情况制定风险限额指标并定期监测指标使用情况、及时报告异常情况。建立合理的市场风险压力测试机制，不断完善压力测试情景，提高市场风险计量水平。建立了包括交易限额、止损限额以及风险限额在内的限额结构体系，实施对市场风险的识别、监测和控制。通过调整资产和负债的期限、设定市场风险限额等手段来管理利率风险。通过优化外汇资产与负债的结构组合，规避汇率风险。采用缺口分析、久期分析及利率风险敏感度分析等方法监控管理资产、负债组合的整体利率风险，利用债券组合收益、市值评估、久期等系列风险指标，加大债券业务风险管控力度。

## （四）操作风险管理对策

根据本行经营战略、管理理念及外部经济发展趋势，操作风险管理以风险识别为切入点，以流程梳理为抓手，将制度分解到各流程环节，并持续、及时地进行流程信息的完善或更新，以推进操作风险管理标准化。通过印发

《案防阻击图使用手册》、构建“案防知识学习平台”、开展案防专题测试，建立起覆盖各类业务全流程、全岗位、系统性、立体式的精细化案防管理体系，进一步充实了操作风险管理手段。

## （五）信息科技风险管理对策

本行扎实践行“科技强行”理念，大力实施金融科技战略，抓好系统平台建设，提升客户体验及服务水平，强化科技赋能业务发展。一是加快推广分布式微服务架构体系，线上化产品研发不断提速，多元化网络渠道不断拓展，场景化平台不断丰富，敏捷开发快速推进信息系统建设。二是继续以大数据智能化建设为抓手，积极推进数据治理和应用水平，标准化数据应用不断整合，充分挖掘数据价值，优化数据模型，为综合营销提供数据支撑。三是严格落实网络安全责任，加强网络重点领域防护，细化网络安全等级保护和关键信息基础设施保护措施，持续开展渗透测试、网络安全评估，组织信息科技专项审计和信息科技外包专项审计。截至报告期末，未发生重大网络安全事件。四是业务连续性体系持续完善，组织重要业务影响分析，实战场景开展业务连续性演练，充分验证应急预案的合理性和有效性。

## (六) 声誉风险管理对策

本行高度重视声誉风险管理工作，坚持将声誉风险融入到公司治理、经营管理及全面风险管理体系中，努力构建组织健全、职责清晰的声誉风险治理架构和相互衔接、有效联动的运行机制，提升声誉风险管理能力和质效。报告期内，全面贯彻落实各项规章制度要求，及时修订声誉风险管理相关制度；严格执行7*24小时舆情监测机制，定期开展潜在声誉风险排查；夯实声誉风险意识文化基础，组织全行专项培训；巩固与各类媒体的合作关系，持续加大正面宣传力度，为全行高质量稳健发展营造良好舆论环境。本行高度重视消费者权益保护工作，成立了消费者权益保护委员会，逐步建立健全了消费者权益保护内部考核机制，将消保工作纳入到全行绩效考核，修订完善了消保工作制度，不断加强员工教育培训，提升消保服务水平，积极开展各类宣传教育活动，妥善处理各类投诉事件。报告期内，受理有效投诉件为10起，投诉内容主要涉及网贷、信用卡、自助设备等范围，通过合理有效处置，投诉事件处理完结率100%。

## （七）合规风险管理对策

本行坚持“合规是发展的基石”“合规创造价值”理念，组织开展了“规范建设提升年”及“内控合规管理建设年”活动，修订完善了216个行内规章制度，借助“网络学院”“合规学堂”“内控合规与操作风险管理系统”等平台，加强制度学习传导，开展合规教育，有效提升员工合规意识。同时，本行建立了监管法规传导机制，每季度印发合规导刊，持续关注法律、规则和准则的最新发展，准确把握法律、规则和准则对银行经营的影响，及时将监管要求转化为本行内部业务制度，定期将监管规定及行内规章制度录入“内控合规与操作风险管理系统”。

## （八）洗钱和恐怖融资风险管理对策

本行持续加强洗钱和恐怖融资风险管控，对洗钱和恐怖融资风险进行持续识别、审慎评估、有效控制及全程管理。通过健全洗钱和恐怖融资风险管理体系，完善风险管理架构，深化内控制度建设，完善反洗钱系统功能，加强反洗钱业务人员培训，持续提高洗钱和恐怖融资风险管控水平。本行严格履行反洗钱和反恐怖融资义务，积极开展反洗钱和反恐怖融资宣传工作，在山东省反洗钱工作联席会议办公室举办的“山东省第一届反洗钱知识网络竞赛暨集中宣传活动”中获得一等奖。

# Part IV Various Risks and Risk Management Strategies

### I. Types and Forms of Risks

The Bank was mainly faced with the following risks in its business: Credit risk, market risk, operational risk, liquidity risk and so on. The Bank positively took various effective measures to strive for preventing and dissolving the above risks.

### II. Risk Management Strategies

The Board of Directors of the Bank and its Risk Management Committee, along with the Senior Management and its Risk Management Committee were in charge of risk management and control of the Bank. A framework system in line with the supervisory requirements of modern banks has been established in various risk management and internal control through continuous coordination and promotion of the project construction of the comprehensive risk management system. According to the division of responsibilities, relevant departments were specifically responsible for the management and control over credit risk, market risk, operational risk, liquidity risk and other risks of the Bank.

#### (I) Countermeasures for credit risk management

The Bank strictly implemented the relevant system and process of credit risk, and implemented the whole process risk management and control of credit risk. According to the annual credit guidance, the service entities were strengthened, the credit investment became more scientific and reasonable, the adjustment and optimization of credit structure was vigorously promoted, all superior resources were concentrated, and the development of inclusive small and micro loans, advanced manufacturing, green credit and other fields was supported. At the same time, the speed of real estate loans and large loans was strictly controlled, and the transformation and development effect of credit business was obvious. By centralizing the collection of credit approval authority, reducing approval nodes, optimizing approval modes, and taking multiple measures to strengthen credit risk prevention and control, the risk management and control of risk sources and key areas were stricter. The disposal of non-performing loans was solidly promoted, and the credit business achieved scientific, steady and sustained development. The Bank incorporated the management of large-amount risk exposure into the comprehensive risk management system, measured and dynamically monitored the change of large-amount risk exposure, and effectively controlled the risk of customer concentration. As of the end of the reporting period, the Bank's non-interbank single customers, non-interbank associated customers, interbank single customers and interbank group customers up to the standard of large-amount risk exposure met the regulatory requirements.

##### (II) Countermeasures for liquidity risk management

The Bank continued to maintain a sound liquidity risk management strategy, attached great importance to the compliance management of liquidity indicators, implemented the monthly management mechanism of key liquidity indicators, planned the total business volume and structure in advance, predicted and analyzed the impact on liquidity indicators, and actively adjusted its business strategy. At the same time, the Bank continued to improve its ability for asset-liability management, expanded the channels of capital sources, ensured the appropriate total amount of liabilities, stable sources and diversified structure, made preparation and financing arrangements in advance, increased the reserve of high-quality liquidity assets, and strengthened the “safety pad” against external impact. The 2021 liquidity risk indicators were generally controlled within a reasonable range, and the main liquidity regulatory indicators met the requirements.

#### (III) Countermeasures for market risk management

The Bank constantly optimized the market risk monitoring and reporting mechanism, regularly analyzed business risks,

strengthened market risk middle control and continued to enhance risk monitoring in the financial market and other relevant businesses by dispatching risk managers. According to the market conditions, the Bank formulated risk limit indicators, regularly monitored the use of indicators, and reported abnormality in a timely manner. The Bank established a reasonable market risk pressure test mechanism, constantly improved the pressure test scenario, and improved its market risk measurement. The Bank established a limit structure system including trading limit, stop loss limit and risk limit to identify, monitor and control market risks. The Bank managed interest rate risk by adjusting the maturity of assets and liabilities and setting market risk limits. The Bank avoided exchange rate risk by optimizing the structural combination of foreign exchange assets and liabilities. The Bank used gap analysis, duration analysis and interest rate risk sensitivity analysis to monitor and manage the overall interest rate risk of asset and liability portfolio, and used a series of risk indicators such as bond portfolio income, market value assessment and duration to strengthen the risk management and control of bond business.

##### (IV) Countermeasures for operational risk management

According to the Bank’s business strategy, management philosophy and external economic development trend, the Bank took risk identification as the breakthrough point and process combing as the starting point in operational risk management, decomposed the system into various process links, and continuously and timely improved or updated process information, so to promote the standardization of operational risk management. By issuing the User Manual of Case Prevention Blocking Map, building a “Case Prevention Knowledge Learning Platform” and carrying out special tests on case prevention, the Bank established a systematic and three-dimensional refined case prevention management system covering the whole process and posts of various businesses, further enriching the operational risk management means.

##### (V) Countermeasures for IT risk management

The Bank practiced the concept of “revitalizing the industry through science and technology”, vigorously implemented the financial technology strategy, paid special attention to the construction of system platform, improved customer experience and service level, and strengthened the development of technology empowerment business. First, the Bank accelerated the promotion of distributed micro-service architecture, accelerated the research and development of online products, expanded diversified network channels, enriched scenario platforms, and rapidly promoted the construction of information systems through agile development. Second, the Bank continued to focus on the intelligent construction of big data, actively promoted the level of data governance and application, continuously integrated standardized data applications, fully exploited data value, optimized data models, and provided data support for comprehensive marketing. Third, the Bank strictly implemented its network security responsibilities, strengthened the protection of key areas of the network, refined the protection measures for network security level and key information infrastructure, continuously carried out penetration tests and network security assessments, and organized special audits of information technology and information technology outsourcing. By the end of the reporting period, no major network security incidents had occurred. Fourth, the business continuity system was continuously improved, and important business impact analysis and business continuity drills were organized in actual scenarios to fully verify the rationality and effectiveness of emergency plans.

##### (VI) Countermeasures for reputational risk management

The Bank attaches great importance to reputational risk management, insists on integrating reputational risk into corporate governance, operation management and comprehensive risk management system, and strives to build a reputational risk governance structure with sound organization and clear responsibilities and an operational mechanism with mutual connection and effective linkage, so as to enhance reputational risk management capability and quality and efficiency. During the reporting period, the Bank fully implemented the requirements of various rules and regulations and revised the relevant systems of reputational risk management in a timely manner; strictly implemented the 7*24-hour public opinion monitoring mechanism and regularly conducted potential reputation risk investigation; consolidated the cultural foundation of reputational risk awareness and organized special training for the

whole bank; consolidate cooperation with various media, continuously increased positive publicity, and created a good public opinion environment for the high-quality and steady development of the whole bank. The Bank attaches great importance to the protection of consumers' rights and interests. The Bank has set up a Consumer Rights Protection Committee, gradually established and improved the internal assessment mechanism for consumer rights protection, incorporated consumer rights protection into the performance assessment of the whole bank, revised and improved the consumer rights protection system, continuously strengthened employee education and training, improved the level of consumer rights protection services, actively carried out various publicity and education activities, and properly handled various complaints. During the reporting period, the Bank accepted 10 valid complaints, mainly involving online loans, credit cards, self-service equipment, etc. Through reasonable and effective disposal, the completion rate of complaint handling was 100%.

##### (VII) Countermeasures for compliance risk management

The Bank adheres to the concept of “compliance is the cornerstone of development” and “compliance creates value”. The Bank organized the activities of “Standardization Construction Promotion Year” and “Internal Control Compliance Management Construction Year”, revised and improved 216 internal rules and regulations, and strengthened system learning and transmission, carried out compliance education, and effectively enhanced employees’ compliance awareness with the help of platforms such as “Network College”, “Compliance School” and “Internal Control Compliance and Operational Risk Management System”. In addition, the Bank has established a transmission mechanism of regulatory laws and regulations. The Bank issues a compliance guide every quarter, continuously pays attention to the latest development of laws, rules and standards, accurately grasps the impact of laws, rules and standards on bank operations, timely transforms regulatory requirements into the internal business system of the Bank, and regularly records regulatory requirements and internal rules and regulations into the “Internal Control Compliance and Operational Risk Management System”.

##### (VIII) Countermeasures for money laundering and terrorist financing risk management

The Bank continued to strengthen the risk management and control of money laundering and terrorist financing, and carried out continuous identification, prudent assessment, effective control and whole-process management for the risk of money laundering and terrorist financing. Through improving the money laundering and terrorist financing risk management system, the Bank improved its risk management structure, deepened the construction of the internal control system, improved the functions of the anti-money laundering system, strengthened the training of anti-money laundering personnel, and continuously improved the level of money laundering and terrorist financing risk control. The Bank strictly fulfilled its obligations on anti-money laundering and anti-terrorist financing, actively carried out anti-money laundering and anti-terrorist financing publicity, and won the first prize in the "First Anti-Money Laundering Knowledge Network Competition and Centralized Publicity Campaign in Shandong Province" organized by the Office of the Joint Conference on Anti-Money Laundering Work in Shandong Province.

## Part V Significant Events of the Year

I. During the reporting period, the Bank has not incurred any litigation and arbitration matters with material effect on its operating activities.

II. During the reporting period, the Bank had no material issues of acquisition and merger by absorption.

On December 30, the Bank held the thirteenth meeting of the seventh Board of Directors and the fourteenth meeting of the seventh Board of Supervisors.

## 第六部分·财务会计报告

## 一、审计报告

#### 审计报告

信会师报字[2022]第ZH30029号

齐商银行股份有限公司全体股东：

## 一、审计意见

我们审计了齐商银行股份有限公司（以下简称齐商银行）财务报表，包括2021年12月31日的合并及母公司资产负债表，2021年度的合并及母公司利润表、合并及母公司现金流量表、合并及母公司股东权益变动表以及相关财务报表附注。

我们认为，后附的财务报表在所有重大方面按照企业会计准则的规定编制，公允反映了齐商银行2021年12月31日的合并及母公司财务状况以及2021年度的合并及母公司经营成果和现金流量。

## 二、形成审计意见的基础

我们按照中国注册会计师审计准则的规定执行了审计工作。审计报告的“注册会计师对财务报表审计的责任”部分进一步阐述了我们在这些准则下的责任。按照中国注册会计师职业道德守则，我们独立于齐商银行，并履行了职业道德方面的其他责任。我们相信，我们获取的审计证据是充分、适当的，为发表审计意见提供了基础。

## 三、管理层和治理层对财务报表的责任

齐商银行管理层（以下简称管理层）负责按照企业会计准则的规定编制财务报表，使其实现公允反映，并设计、执行和维护必要的内部控制，以使财务报表不存在由于舞弊或错误导致的重大错报。

在编制财务报表时，管理层负责评估齐商银行的持续经营能力，披露与持续经营相关的事项（如适用），并运用持续经营假设，除非计划进行清算、终止运营或别无其他现实的选择。

治理层负责监督齐商银行的财务报告过程。

## 四、注册会计师对财务报表审计的责任

我们的目标是对财务报表整体是否不存在由于舞弊或错误导致的重大错报获取合理保证，并出具包含审计意见的审计报告。合理保证是高水平的保证，但并不能保证按照审计准则执行的审计在某一重大错报存在时总能发现。错报可能由于舞弊或错误导致，如果合理预期错报单独或汇总起来可能影响财务报表使用者依据财务报表作出的经济决策，则通常认为错报是重大的。

在按照审计准则执行审计工作的过程中，我们运用职业判断，并保持职业怀疑。同时，我们也执行以下工作：

（1）识别和评估由于舞弊或错误导致的财务报表重大错报风险，设计和实施审计程序以应对这些风险，并获取充分、适当的审计证据，作为发表审计意见的基础。由于舞弊可能涉及串通、伪造、故意遗漏、虚假陈述或凌驾于内部控制之上，未能发现由于舞弊导致的重大错报的风险高于未能发现由于错误导致的重大错报的风险。

(2) 了解与审计相关的内部控制，以设计恰当的审计程序，但目的并非对内部控制的有效性发表意见。

(3) 评价管理层选用会计政策的恰当性和作出会计估计及相关披露的合理性。

（4）对管理层使用持续经营假设的恰当性得出结论。同时，根据获取的审计证据，就可能导致对齐商银行持续经营能力产生重大疑虑的事项或情况是否存在重大不确定性得出结论。如果我们得出结论认为存在重大不确定性，审计准则要求我们在审计报告中提请报表使用者注意财务报表中的相关披露；如果披露不充分，我们应当发表非无保留意见。我们的结论基于截至审计报告日可获得的信息。然而，未来的事项或情况可能导致齐商银行不

能持续经营。

(5) 评价财务报表的总体列报（包括披露）、结构和内容，并评价财务报表是否公允反映相关交易和事项。

（6）就齐商银行中实体或业务活动的财务信息获取充分、适当的审计证据，以对合并财务报表发表审计意见。我们负责指导、监督和执行集团审计，并对审计意见承担全部责任。

我们与治理层就计划的审计范围、时间安排和重大审计发现等事项进行沟通，包括沟通我们在审计中识别出的值得关注的内部控制缺陷。

## 三、财务报表附注

## 一、公司基本情况

## (一) 公司概况

齐商银行股份有限公司（以下简称“本公司”或“齐商银行”）是1997年8月在城市信用合作社基础上成立并在山东省工商局注册的股份制金融企业。本公司原名淄博市商业银行股份有限公司，于2009年2月13日，经中国银监会批准更名为齐商银行股份有限公司。

本公司法定代表人：李大鹏；注册地址：淄博市张店区中心路105号；金融许可证号：B0171H237030001号；企业统一社会信用代码：913700001641139094。

经营范围主要包括：吸收公众存款；发放短期、中期和长期贷款；办理国内结算；办理票据承兑与贴现；发行金融债券；从事同业拆借；提供担保、代理保险、房屋租赁等；外汇存款；外汇贷款；外汇汇款；外币兑换；国际结算；同业外汇拆借；外汇票据的承兑和贴现；外汇借款；外汇担保；结汇、售汇；资信调查、咨询、鉴证业务。以及其他经中国银行业监督管理委员会依照有关法律、行政法规和其他规定批准的业务。

## （二）合并财务报表范围

至 2021 年 12 月 31 日，本公司合并财务报表范围内子公司如下：


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>子公司名称</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>临沂河东齐商村镇银行股份有限公司</td></tr></table>

临沂河东齐商村镇银行股份有限公司

## 二、财务报表的编制基础

## (一) 编制基础

公司以持续经营为基础，根据实际发生的交易和事项，按照财政部颁布的《企业会计准则——基本准则》和具体会计准则、企业会计准则应用指南、企业会计准则解释及其他相关规定（以下合称“企业会计准则”）编制财务报表。

(二) 持续经营

公司自本报告期末至少12个月内具备持续经营能力，无影响持续经营能力的重大事项。

## 三、重要会计政策及会计估计

## （一）遵循企业会计准则的声明

公司所编制的财务报表符合企业会计准则的要求，真实、完整地反映了报告期公司的财务状况、经营成果、现金流量等有关信息。

(二) 会计期间

自公历1月1日起至12月31日止为一个会计年度。

(三) 营业周期

本公司营业周期为 12 个月。

（四）记账本位币

本公司采用人民币为记账本位币。

（五）以下注释项目除特别注明之外，金额单位为人民币元。

(六) 合并财务报表的编制方法

## 1. 合并范围

本公司合并财务报表的合并范围以控制为基础确定，所有子公司（包括本公司所控制的单独主体）均纳入合并财务报表。

## 2. 合并程序

本公司以自身和各子公司的财务报表为基础，根据其他有关资料，编制合并财务报表。本公司编制合并财务报表，将整个企业集团视为一个会计主体，依据相关企业会计准则的确认、计量和列报要求，按照统一的会计政策，反映本公司集团整体财务状况、经营成果和现金流量。

所有纳入合并财务报表合并范围的子公司所采用的会计政策、会计期间与本公司一致。

### （七）现金及现金等价物

在编制现金流量表时，将本公司库存现金以及可以随时用于支付的存款确认为现金。

将同时具备期限短（从购买日起三个月内到期）、流动性强、易于转换为已知现金、价值变动风险很小四个条件的投资，确定为现金等价物。包括存放中央银行款项（扣除法定存款准备金）、存放同业款项、拆出资金、买入返售金融资产。

### （八）外币业务和外币报表折算

## 1. 外币业务

外币业务采用交易发生日的即期汇率作为折算汇率将外币金额折合成人民币记账。

资产负债表日外币货币性项目余额按资产负债表日即期汇率折算，由此产生的汇兑差额，除属于与购建符合资本化条件的资产相关的外币专门借款产生的汇兑差额按照借款费用资本化的原则处理外，均计入当期损益。

## 2. 外币财务报表的折算

资产负债表中的资产和负债项目，采用资产负债表日的即期汇率折算；所有者权益项目除“未分配利润”项目外，其他项目采用发生时的即期汇率折算。利润表中的收入和费用项目，采用交易发生日的即期汇率折算。

### (九) 金融工具

## 1. 金融工具的分类

根据本行管理金融资产的业务模式和金融资产的合同现金流量特征，金融资产于初始确认时分类为：以摊余成本计量的金融资产、以公允价值计量且其变动计入其他综合收益的金融资产（债务工具）和以公允价值计量且其变动计入当期损益的金融资产。

## 2. 金融工具的确认依据和计量方法

#### (1) 确认依据

本行于成为金融工具合同的一方时确认一项金融资产或金融负债。

满足下列条件的，终止确认金融资产（或金融资产的一部分，或一组类似金融资产的一部分），即从其账户和资产负债表内予以转销：

##### ①收取金融资产现金流量的权利届满；

②转移了收取金融资产现金流量的权利，或在“过手”协议下承担了及时将收取的现金流量全额支付给第三方的义务；并且(i)实质上转让了金融资产所有权上几乎所有的风险和报酬，或(ii)虽然实质上既没有转移也没有保留金融资产所有权上几乎所有的风险和报酬，但放弃了对该金融资产的控制。

#### (2) 计量方法

##### ①以摊余成本计量的金融资产

以摊余成本计量的金融资产包括存放中央银行款项、存放同业款项、拆出资金、买入返售金融资产、应收利息、发放贷款和垫款、债权投资等，按公允价值进行初始计量，相关交易费用计入初始确认金额。

持有期间采用实际利率法计算的利息计入当期损益。

收回或处置时，将取得的价款与该金融资产账面价值之间的差额计入当期损益。

②以公允价值计量且其变动计入其他综合收益的金融资产（债务工具）

以公允价值计量且其变动计入其他综合收益的金融资产（债务工具）包括其他债权投资等，按公允价值进行初始计量，相关交易费用计入初始确认金额。该金融资产按公允价值进行后续计量，公允价值变动除采用实际利率法计算的利息、减值损失或利得和汇兑损益之外，均计入其他综合收益。

终止确认时，之前计入其他综合收益的累计利得或损失从其他综合收益中转出，计入当期损益。

③以公允价值计量且其变动计入其他综合收益的金融资产（权益工具）

以公允价值计量且其变动计入其他综合收益的金融资产（权益工具）包括其他权益工具投资等，按公允价值进行初始计量，相关交易费用计入初始确认金额。该金融资产按公允价值进行后续计量，公允价值变动计入其他综合收益。取得的股利计入当期损益。

终止确认时，之前计入其他综合收益的累计利得或损失从其他综合收益中转出，计入留存收益。

④以公允价值计量且其变动计入当期损益的金融资产

以公允价值计量且其变动计入当期损益的金融资产包括交易性金融资产、衍生金融资产等，按公允价值进行初始计量，相关交易费用计入当期损益。该金融资产按公允价值进行后续计量，公允价值变动计入当期损益。

终止确认时，其公允价值与初始入账金额之间的差额确认为投资收益，同时调整公允价值变动损益。

⑤以公允价值计量且其变动计入当期损益的金融负债

以公允价值计量且其变动计入当期损益的金融负债包括交易性金融负债、衍生金融负债等，按公允价值进行初始计量，相关交易费用计入当期损益。该金融负债按公允价值进行后续计量，公允价值变动计入当期损益。

终止确认时，其公允价值与初始入账金额之间的差额确认为投资收益，同时调整公允价值变动损益。

⑥以摊余成本计量的金融负债

以摊余成本计量的金融负债包括向中央银行借款、同业及其他金融机构存放款项、拆入资金、卖出回购金融资产款、吸收存款、应付利息、应付债券等，按公允价值进行初始计量，相关交易费用计入初始确认金额。

持有期间采用实际利率法计算的利息计入当期损益。

终止确认时，将支付的对价与该金融负债账面价值之间的差额计入当期损益。

## 3. 金融资产转移的确认依据和计量方法

本行发生金融资产转移时，如已将金融资产所有权上几乎所有的风险和报酬转移给转入方，则终止确认该金融资产；如保留了金融资产所有权上几乎所有的风险和报酬的，则不终止确认该金融资产。

在判断金融资产转移是否满足上述金融资产终止确认条件时，采用实质重于形式的原则。本行将金融资产转移区分为金融资产整体转移和部分转移。金融资产整体转移满足终止确认条件的，将下列两项金额的差额计入当期损益：

(1) 所转移金融资产的账面价值；

(2) 因转移而收到的对价，与原直接计入所有者权益的公允价值变动累计额之和。

金融资产部分转移满足终止确认条件的，将所转移金融资产整体的账面价值，在终止确认部分和未终止确认部分之间，按照各自的相对公允价值进行分摊，并将下列两项金额的差额计入当期损益：

①终止确认部分的账面价值；

②终止确认部分的对价，与原直接计入所有者权益的公允价值变动累计额中对应终止确认部分的金额之和。金融资产转移不满足终止确认条件的，继续确认该金融资产，所收到的对价确认为一项金融负债。

## 4. 金融负债终止确认条件

金融负债的现时义务全部或部分已经解除的，则终止确认该金融负债或其一部分；本行若与债权人签定协议，以承担新金融负债方式替换现存金融负债，且新金融负债与现存金融负债的合同条款实质上不同的，则终止确认现存金融负债，并同时确认新金融负债。

对现存金融负债全部或部分合同条款作出实质性修改的，则终止确认现存金融负债或其一部分，同时将修改条款后的金融负债确认为一项新金融负债。

金融负债全部或部分终止确认时，终止确认的金融负债账面价值与支付对价（包括转出的非现金资产或承担的新金融负债）之间的差额，计入当期损益。

本行若回购部分金融负债的，在回购日按照继续确认部分与终止确认部分的相对公允价值，将该金融负债整体的账面价值进行分配。分配给终止确认部分的账面价值与支付的对价（包括转出的非现金资产或承担的新金融负债）之间的差额，计入当期损益。

## 5. 金融资产和金融负债的公允价值的确定方法

存在活跃市场的金融工具，以活跃市场中的报价确定其公允价值。不存在活跃市场的金融工具，采用估值技术确定其公允价值。在估值时，本行采用在当前情况下适用并且有足够可利用数据和其他信息支持的估值技术，选择与市场参与者在相关资产或负债的交易中所考虑的资产或负债特征相一致的输入值，并优先使用相关可观察输入值。只有在相关可观察输入值无法取得或取得不切实可行的情况下，才使用不可观察输入值。

6. 金融资产减值

对于以摊余成本计量和以公允价值计量且其变动计入其他综合收益的金融资产（债务工具），以及部分贷款承诺和财务担保合同，本行结合前瞻性信息进行预期信用损失评估。

对于纳入预期信用损失计量的金融工具，本行评估相关金融工具的信用风险自初始确认后是否已显著增加，运用“三阶段”减值模型分别计量其损失准备、确认预期信用损失：

(1) 阶段一：自初始确认后信用风险并未显著增加的金融工具。

(2) 阶段二：自初始确认后信用风险显著增加，但并未将其视为已发生信用减值的金融工具。

(3) 阶段三：已发生信用减值的金融工具。

阶段一金融工具按照相当于该金融工具未来12个月内预期信用损失的金额计量其损失准备，阶段二和阶段三金融工具按照相当于该金融工具整个存续期内预期信用损失的金额计量其损失准备。

以公允价值计量且其变动计入其他综合收益的金融资产（债务工具），在其他综合收益中确认其损失准备，并将减值损失或利得计入当期损益，不减少该金融资产在资产负债表中列示的账面价值。

在前一会计期间已经按照相当于金融工具整个存续期内预期信用损失的金额计量了损失准备，但在当期资产负债表日，该金融工具已不再属于自初始确认后信用风险显著增加的情形的，在当期资产负债表日按照相当于未来12个月内预期信用损失的金额计量该金融工具的损失准备，由此形成的损失准备的转回金额作为减值利得计入当期损益；但购买或源生的已发生信用减值的金融资产除外。对于购买或源生的已发生信用减值的金融资产，在当期资产负债日仅将自初始确认后整个存续期内预期信用损失的累计变动确认为损失准备。

## 7. 财务担保合同及贷款承诺

财务担保合同要求提供者为合同持有人提供偿还保障，即在被担保人到期不能履行合同条款支付款项时，代为偿付合同持有人的损失。

财务担保合同在初始确认时按照公允价值计量，除指定为以公允价值计量且其变动计入当期损益的金融负债的财务担保合同外，其余财务担保合同在初始确认后按照资产负债表日确定的预期信用损失准备金额和初始确认金额扣除按照收入确认原则确定的累积摊销额后的余额两者孰高进行后续计量。

贷款承诺是本行向客户提供的一项在承诺期间内以既定的合同条款向客户发放贷款的承诺。贷款承诺按照预期信用损失模型计提减值损失。

本行将财务担保合同和贷款承诺的减值准备列报在预计负债中。

### (十) 长期股权投资

## 1. 共同控制、重大影响的判断标准

共同控制，是指按照相关约定对某项安排所共有的控制，并且该安排的相关活动必须经过分享控制权的参与方一致同意后才能决策。本公司与其他合营方一同对被投资单位实施共同控制且对被投资单位净资产享有权利的，被投资单位为本公司的合营企业。

重大影响，是指对一个企业的财务和经营决策有参与决策的权力，但并不能够控制或者与其他方一起共同控制这些政策的制定。本能够对被投资单位施加重大影响的，被投资单位为本公司联营企业。

## 2. 初始投资成本的确定

#### (1) 企业合并形成的长期股权投资

同一控制下的企业合并：公司以支付现金、转让非现金资产或承担债务方式以及以发行权益性证券作为合并对价的，在合并日按照取得被合并方所有者权益在最终控制方合并财务报表中的账面价值的份额作为长期股权投资的初始投资成本。

##### (2) 其他方式取得的长期股权投资

以支付现金方式取得的长期股权投资，按照实际支付的购买价款作为初始投资成本。

以发行权益性证券取得的长期股权投资，按照发行权益性证券的公允价值作为初始投资成本。

## 3. 后续计量及损益确认方法

#### (1) 成本法核算的长期股权投资

公司对子公司的长期股权投资，采用成本法核算。除取得投资时实际支付的价款或对价中包含的已宣告但尚未发放的现金股利或利润外，公司按照享有被投资单位宣告发放的现金股利或利润确认当期投资收益。

#### (2) 权益法核算的长期股权投资

对联营企业和合营企业的长期股权投资，采用权益法核算。初始投资成本大于投资时应享有被投资单位可辨认净资产公允价值份额的差额，不调整长期股权投资的初始投资成本；初始投资成本小于投资时应享有被投资单位可辨认净资产公允价值份额的差额，不调整长期股权投资的初始投资成本；初始投资成本小于投资时应享有被投资单位可辨认净资产公允价值份额的差额，不调整长期股权投资的初始投资成本。

位可辨认净资产公允价值份额的差额，计入当期损益。

### (十一) 固定资产

## 1. 固定资产确认条件

固定资产指为生产商品、提供劳务、出租或经营管理而持有，并且使用寿命超过一个会计年度的有形资产。固定资产在同时满足下列条件时予以确认：

(1) 与该固定资产有关的经济利益很可能流入企业；

(2) 该固定资产的成本能够可靠地计量。

## 2. 各类固定资产的折旧方法

固定资产折旧采用年限平均法分类计提，根据固定资产类别、预计使用寿命和预计净残值率确定折旧率。如固定资产各组成部分的使用寿命不同或者以不同方式为企业提供经济利益，则选择不同折旧率或折旧方法，分别计提折旧。

融资租赁方式租入的固定资产，能合理确定租赁期届满时将会取得租赁资产所有权的，在租赁资产尚可使用年限内计提折旧；无法合理确定租赁期届满时能够取得租赁资产所有权的，在租赁期与租赁资产尚可使用年限两者中较短的期间内计提折旧。

主要固定资产类别的折旧年限如下：
